### [1] [Personalized and Explainable Blood Pressure Estimation from PPG via Hybrid CNN--Morphological Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13190)
*Myung-Kyu Yi,Jongshill Lee,Jeyeon Lee,In Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种生理引导的混合框架，将CNN波形分支与形态学先验分支结合，用于个性化无袖带血压估计，在MIMIC-III子集上实现SBP MAE 3.77 mmHg、DBP MAE 2.36 mmHg，并提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPG无袖带血压监测存在两大局限：基于手工特征的方法依赖精确基准点检测且仅适用于短期分析；深度学习模型虽精度高但多为黑箱，生理可解释性不足。作者希望在提升个性化精度的同时增强可解释性，并降低对大规模训练数据的依赖。

Method: 提出生理引导的混合学习框架：一支CNN分支捕捉全局与局部波形动态，另一支形态学先验分支显式编码个体血管特征；嵌入基于形态学的特征集以编码个体血管特性，实现个性化并减少对大规模数据集的依赖。在MIMIC-III数据库子集上采用受试者特定（个性化）测试协议进行评估，并使用SHAP进行可解释性分析。

Result: 在受试者特定测试协议下，收缩压MAE为3.77 mmHg、舒张压MAE为2.36 mmHg，相比仅用CNN的个性化基线分别相对改善43.7%和32.4%。SHAP分析证实引入的形态学先验特征与个体血管特征一致，增强了逐受试者可解释性。

Conclusion: 个性化、生理引导的混合学习结合新型形态学描述符，有望在真实场景中实现准确且可解释的血压监测。

Abstract: Continuous cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring using photoplethysmography (PPG) offers a promising solution for personalized healthcare. However, existing methods have two major limitations. Handcrafted feature-based approaches rely on precise fiducial point detection and are limited to short-term analysis, while deep learning models, despite their accuracy, often operate as black boxes with limited physiological interpretability. To address these challenges, we propose a physiology-guided hybrid framework for personalized BP estimation that couples a convolutional neural network (CNN) branch capturing global and local waveform dynamics with a morphology-prior branch that explicitly encodes person-specific vascular characteristics. By embedding a morphology-based feature set that explicitly encodes individual vascular characteristics, the proposed framework enhances personalization and reduces dependence on large-scale training datasets. Evaluated on a subset of the MIMIC-III database under a subject-specific (personalized) testing protocol, the proposed personalized physiology-guided hybrid approach achieved mean absolute errors (MAEs) of 3.77 mmHg for systolic BP and 2.36 mmHg for diastolic BP, corresponding to relative improvements of 43.7% and 32.4% over a subject-specific (personalized) CNN-only baseline. SHAP-based analysis confirmed that the introduced morphology-prior features align with individual vascular characteristics, reinforcing per-subject interpretability. These findings highlight the potential of personalized, physiology-guided hybrid learning with novel morphological descriptors for accurate and explainable BP monitoring in real-world settings.

</details>


### [2] [Part Grounding, Not Action Knowledge: Locating the Bottleneck in VLM Affordance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13225)
*Sarthak Sattigeri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示视觉语言模型在低级操作推理中的主要瓶颈是"部件定位"而非"动作知识"：模型往往描述与评分目标不同的部件；在提示中明确目标部件名称后，动作准确率与push召回率大幅提升，且所有模型均超过恒定基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准测试普遍认为视觉语言模型对低级操作推理能力差，但总体准确率分数无法指出具体是哪一步失败。本文旨在拆分可操作性（affordance）问题中被混淆的两个步骤：识别物体上应作用的部件，以及知道该部件需要何种动作，从而定位真正的瓶颈。

Method: 在19个铰接物体上，向来自三家开发者的八个模型提问机器人应施加何种运动。设置开放提示与"命名目标部件"提示两种条件进行对比；同时用无标签集的自由文本让模型描述同一部件；并在真实照片与渲染物体上测试抓取点定位能力。此外通过将自身数值与平凡替代方案对比，检查阈值与标注规则中的测量误差。

Result: 开放提示下，在64次push本应为正确答案的评估中仅出现1次；尽管push对19个物体中的8个正确且每次都出现在候选标签集里。输出检查显示模型描述的是与评分部件不同的部件（如讲解如何拿起相机而非按按钮）。命名目标部件后，所有模型动作准确率由0.158–0.474提升至0.684–0.947（提升0.32–0.63），push召回率由0–1/8提升至7–8/8。开放提示下无模型能超过忽略图像的恒定答案；命名部件后八个模型全部超过。无标签集自由描述时，模型对8个中的6–8个产出按压类语言。真实照片上仅三/八个模型抓取点定位优于恒定基线，渲染物体上无一模型优于基线。还发现两处自身测量误差：一个使恒定基线得0.929的阈值，以及一个在19个物体中4个标注错误的规则。

Conclusion: 结果难以用缺失动作知识解释，而指向部件定位是主导瓶颈，该模式在三个模型家族中一致且不随能力提升而减弱。命名部件提供了定位变量，因此这界定的是完美部件检测器所能带来的上限，而非证明存在通用的力学模型。

Abstract: Benchmarks agree that vision-language models reason poorly about low-level manipulation, but an aggregate accuracy score does not say which step fails. We separate two steps that affordance questions conflate: identifying which part of an object to act on, and knowing what action that part requires. Across 19 articulated objects we asked eight models, spanning three developers, what motion a robot should apply. Under an open prompt, push was produced once in 64 evaluations where it was correct, despite being correct for 8 of 19 objects and appearing in the offered label set every time. Inspecting the outputs showed why: models described a different part than the one being scored, e.g. explaining how to pick up a camera rather than press its button. Naming the target part raises action accuracy by 0.32 to 0.63 for every model, from 0.158-0.474 to 0.684-0.947, and push recall from 0-1/8 to 7-8/8. No model beats a constant answer that ignores the image under the open prompt; once the part is named, all eight do. Asked to describe the same part in free prose with no label set, models produce pressing language for 6 to 8 of 8. These results are hard to reconcile with missing action knowledge, and instead point to part grounding as the dominant bottleneck, a pattern that holds across all three model families and does not diminish with capability. Naming the part supplies the grounding variable, so this bounds what a perfect part detector would offer rather than demonstrating a general model of mechanics. Two supporting results agree: on real photographs only three of eight models localize grasp points better than a constant baseline, and on rendered objects none do. We also document two measurement errors of our own, a threshold that let a constant baseline score 0.929 and a labelling rule wrong on 4 of 19 objects, both caught only by testing our numbers against trivial alternatives.

</details>


### [3] [Synthetic Leprosy Image Generation Using Mask-Conditioned Latent Diffusion and Transfer Learning from Large Chronic Wound Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13226)
*Yusuf Abdulkadir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究构建"分割—潜在扩散—微调"三阶段管线，将慢性创面摄影知识迁移到麻风病皮损图像合成，证明低数据场景下的瓶颈是语义控制而非图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被忽视热带病（如麻风病）的公开标注图像仅数百张，远低于生成模型的数据需求，因此制约机器学习的瓶颈是数据而非算法。作者希望验证：在数据丰富的慢性创面摄影上训练的模型能否迁移到这种低数据场景。

Method: 三阶段流程：（1）用DeepLabV3-ResNet50分割网络（验证集Dice 0.876，IoU 0.799）为两个缺少掩码的创面数据集生成病灶掩码；（2）基于Stable Diffusion 1.5组件构建掩码条件潜在扩散模型，在3,280张感兴趣区域创面裁剪图上训练，将UNet输入卷积通道由4扩至11，以接收三张掩码特征图与一张模糊低频上下文潜变量；（3）在来自约150名患者、764张图像的708对麻风病图像—掩码上微调。采用LPIPS感知距离评估，并以同样242张锚点图像上的真实—真实基线作为参照。

Result: 生成集无模式坍塌：其内部感知多样性（0.662）与真实麻风病集（0.672，95% CI [0.664, 0.680]）在统计上不可区分。生成图像距真实分布0.044 LPIPS，可测但不足一个标准差的一半。微调对输出分布的偏移仅边际，原因被追溯为病灶几何仅通过输入拼接进入网络。

Conclusion: 慢性创面摄影可作为麻风病病灶合成的可行供体域：低层外观迁移良好，剩余障碍在于语义控制而非图像质量。

Abstract: Machine learning for neglected tropical diseases is limited by data, not algorithms: public annotated image sets for leprosy (Hansen's disease) number in the hundreds, orders of magnitude below what generative models require. We ask whether a model trained on abundant chronic wound photography transfers to this low-data regime. We build a three-stage pipeline. First, a DeepLabV3-ResNet50 segmentation network (validation Dice 0.876, IoU 0.799) supplies lesion masks for two wound datasets that ship without them. Second, we assemble a mask-conditioned latent diffusion model from Stable Diffusion 1.5 components and train it on 3,280 region-of-interest wound crops, widening the UNet input convolution from 4 to 11 channels to admit three mask feature maps and a blurred low-frequency context latent. Third, we fine-tune this model on 708 leprosy image-mask pairs drawn from 764 images of approximately 150 patients. We evaluate with LPIPS perceptual distance, anchored by a real-versus-real baseline computed on the same 242 anchor images as the cross-set comparisons; without that reference the cross-set distances cannot be interpreted. The generated set shows no mode collapse: its internal perceptual diversity (0.662) is statistically indistinguishable from that of the real leprosy set (0.672, 95% CI [0.664, 0.680]). Generated images sit 0.044 LPIPS outside the real distribution - measurably apart, but under half of one standard deviation. Fine-tuning shifted the output distribution only marginally, which we trace to lesion geometry reaching the network through input concatenation alone. Chronic wound photography is therefore a viable donor domain for leprosy lesion synthesis: low-level appearance transfers well, and the remaining barrier is semantic control rather than image quality.

</details>


### [4] [What Does the Encoder Actually Decide? A Controlled Comparison of Vision Backbones on Joint Tree Segmentation and Stereo Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13232)
*Yida Lin,Bing Xue,Mengjie Zhang,Sam Schofield,Richard Green*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在固定数据集、解码器、损失、训练计划与评测的前提下，本文构建共享编码器的语义分割与立体深度联合网络，对比约25M量级的[N]个编码器（覆盖CNN、Transformer、混合、MLP-mixer、状态空间模型），考察编码器选择对细密植被上分割与深度的影响。结论：卷积与混合架构最优，Transformer（尤其纯ViT）从零训练易失效；参数量不预示质量；两任务排序高度一致（Spearman ρ=[RhoValue]），无任务冲突；[K]/[N]编码器坍缩为全树分割，需用边界F1而非区域IoU才能暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人树木修剪需要逐像素的"是否属于树"与"距离"两类信息。现有实践通常凭"声誉"而非实测来选定视觉骨干，且分割与深度常由独立任务头承担，导致编码器选择对细密植被（如树枝）上联合任务性能的影响缺乏受控、可量化的结论。作者希望在严格固定其余变量的条件下，量化"仅更换编码器"带来的性能差异，并检验任务间是否存在冲突。

Method: 设计强参数共享网络：单一编码器同时馈入分割与深度两个分支，仅替换编码器，下游不做再调优。在约25M参数预算下，从零训练[N]个编码器，覆盖[M]个架构族（CNN、Transformer、混合、MLP-mixer、状态空间模型）。深度仅在树木像素上评测；分割采用边界F1与背景IoU，以规避"全标为树"的捷径。以Spearman ρ衡量两任务编码器排序的一致性。

Result: 1) 最强编码器为卷积与混合架构而非Transformer：[BestEncoder]取得[MIoU]的分割mIoU与[Delta]的深度δ₁，而[Y]个纯视觉Transformer中有[X]个从零训练时性能崩溃。2) 参数量不能预测质量：[SmallEncoder]仅[P]M参数即超越大其两个数量级的模型。3) 分割与深度排序高度一致（Spearman ρ=[RhoValue]），表明两任务不存在冲突。4) [N]个编码器中有[K]个坍缩为退化的全树分割，该问题被边界F1暴露，却被区域IoU掩盖。

Conclusion: 在细密植被的联合分割—深度任务中，编码器选择的影响显著且不可由参数量或架构"声誉"预测；卷积/混合骨干优于纯Transformer，从零训练时尤为明显。分割与深度可共享编码器而无任务冲突，但评测必须纳入边界F1等对退化预测敏感的指标，以避免被区域IoU掩盖的"全树"坍缩。实践上应以实测而非声望选骨干，并警惕大模型≠更优。

Abstract: A robot pruning trees needs two facts per pixel: whether it belongs to a tree, and its distance. Both are usually obtained via task heads attached to a vision backbone chosen by reputation rather than measurement. Holding dataset, decoders, losses, schedule, and evaluation fixed, we ask: how much does the encoder choice change joint semantic segmentation and stereo depth on thin vegetation? We build a hard parameter-sharing network with one encoder feeding both branches, swapping only the encoder without downstream retuning. We evaluate [N] encoders across [M] architecture families (CNNs, transformers, hybrids, MLP-mixers, state-space models) near a ~25M budget, trained from scratch. Depth is evaluated on tree pixels only; segmentation uses boundary F1 and background IoU to prevent "label-everything-tree" shortcuts. Three findings stand out. First, the strongest encoders are convolutional and hybrid, not transformers: [BestEncoder] leads with [MIoU] segmentation mIoU and [Delta] depth $δ_1$, while [X] of [Y] plain vision transformers collapse when trained from scratch. Second, parameter count does not predict quality --- [SmallEncoder] at only [P]M parameters outranks models two orders of magnitude larger. Third, segmentation and depth rankings agree strongly (Spearman $ρ$ = [RhoValue]), showing no task conflict. Finally, [K] of [N] encoders collapse to degenerate all-tree segmentation --- exposed by boundary F1 but hidden by region IoU.

</details>


### [5] [EMCStereo: Attention-Enhanced Stereo Matching for Thin-Structure Depth Estimation with a Synthetic Tree-Branch Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13233)
*Yida Lin,Bing Xue,Mengjie Zhang,Sam Schofield,Richard Green*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出EMCStereo立体匹配网络（融合EMA、MSFblock、CoordAtt三种轻量注意力模块）与VirtualTree合成数据集（Unreal Engine 5渲染5520对细枝结构立体图像），并建立0.009 px的八路消融噪声基线。EMCStereo在VirtualTree测试集达1.31 px EPE（5.96% D1-all），SceneFlow 1.00 px，KITTI 2012/2015、ETH3D、Middlebury分别为0.80/0.73/0.62/3.19 px，depth accuracy delta_1达92.6%–98.7%；相对噪声基线，注意力堆叠在100 epoch预算下精度中性，MSFblock与CoordAtt在无EMA时带来0.03–0.05 px代价，且网络缩小2.0%、推理时间仅增1.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细结构（如树枝）是立体匹配中最困难的场景之一：树枝仅数像素宽、背景杂乱，且真实树枝的稠密真值几乎无法人工标注。现有数据集与网络在该类细结构上缺乏可靠真值与精度评估手段。

Method: 1) EMCStereo：在PSMNet风格代价体骨干中集成三种轻量注意力模块——对深层语义特征施加Efficient Multi-scale Attention (EMA)，用Multi-Scale Fusion block (MSFblock)以学习空间金字塔权重替代简单拼接，对最终匹配特征施加Coordinate Attention (CoordAtt)；MSFblock将四个金字塔分支压缩为一个。2) VirtualTree：基于Unreal Engine 5与模拟ZED Mini双目配置渲染的合成立体数据集，含5,520对带精确视差标注的细枝图像。3) 八路消融实验，建立0.009 px EPE的运行间噪声基线。

Result: EMCStereo在VirtualTree测试集取得1.31 px EPE（5.96% D1-all），SceneFlow 1.00 px，KITTI 2012、KITTI 2015、ETH3D、Middlebury分别为0.80、0.73、0.62、3.19 px；depth accuracy delta_1为92.6%–98.7%。模块使网络缩小2.0%，推理时间仅增加1.7%。相对0.009 px噪声基线，注意力堆叠在100-epoch预算下为精度中性；MSFblock与CoordAtt在缺少EMA时带来0.03–0.05 px误差代价。

Conclusion: 所提轻量注意力组合在几乎不增加模型规模与推理开销的前提下，于细结构合成数据及主流基准上取得有竞争力精度，且精度提升处于噪声基线量级，说明其贡献主要在于提供细结构可标注的合成数据与可复现的噪声基准，而非显著精度增益。

Abstract: Thin structures such as tree branches are among the hardest cases for stereo matching: a branch is only a few pixels wide, the background is cluttered, and dense ground truth for real branches is nearly impossible to label by hand. We make three contributions. First, EMCStereo integrates three lightweight attention modules into a PSMNet-style cost-volume backbone: Efficient Multi-scale Attention (EMA) on deep semantic features, a Multi-Scale Fusion block (MSFblock) learning spatial pyramid weights instead of concatenating them, and Coordinate Attention (CoordAtt) on final matching features. Because MSFblock collapses four pyramid branches into one, the modules leave the network 2.0% smaller and add only 1.7% inference time overhead. Second, VirtualTree is a synthetic stereo dataset rendered in Unreal Engine 5 with a simulated ZED Mini rig, providing 5,520 pairs with exact disparity for thin branches. Third, an eight-way ablation establishes a run-to-run noise floor of 0.009 px end-point error (EPE). EMCStereo achieves 1.31 px EPE (5.96% D1-all) on the VirtualTree test split, 1.00 px on SceneFlow, and 0.80, 0.73, 0.62, and 3.19 px on KITTI 2012, KITTI 2015, ETH3D, and Middlebury, with depth accuracy delta_1 from 92.6% to 98.7%. Evaluated against the noise floor, the attention stack is accuracy-neutral at a 100-epoch budget, while MSFblock and CoordAtt cost 0.03-0.05 px unless EMA is present.

</details>


### [6] [Pixel-wise Planarity for High-Precision Monocular Plane Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13246)
*Ahmetcan Yavuz,Alpay Ozkan,Rémi Pautrat,Shaohui Liu,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出逐像素平面性预测框架，结合单目深度与法向骨干网络，实现稳健的单目平面分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张RGB图像进行平面分割面临区域分组不精确与几何一致性监督不足的问题，易导致过分割和错误平面检测。

Method: 基于预训练单目几何骨干网络预测深度与表面法向，引入专门的平面性头估计逐像素平面性置信度；推理时将深度、法向与平面性结合，通过轻量区域生长过程在形成平面分割时强制几何一致性；并分析现有平面真值标注的几何不一致性。

Result: 在严格距离阈值下发现现有平面真值标注存在显著几何不一致；在多个数据集上相比先前最先进方法提升了几何精度与分割质量，同时提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: 逐像素平面性预测与几何一致性约束能有效改善单目平面分割的精度、质量与效率，代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Plane segmentation from a single RGB image remains challenging due to imprecise region grouping and geometrically inconsistent supervision, often leading to over-segmentation and false planar detections. We propose instead a pixel-wise planarity prediction framework for robust monocular plane segmentation. Building on a pretrained monocular geometric backbone predicting depth and surface normals, we introduce a dedicated planarity head that estimates per-pixel planarity confidence. During inference, predicted depth, normals, and planarity are combined in a lightweight region-growing procedure that enforces geometric consistency when forming plane segments. We further analyze existing plane ground-truth annotations and demonstrate substantial geometric inconsistencies under strict distance thresholds. Across multiple datasets, our method achieves improved geometric precision and segmentation quality compared to prior state-of-the-art approaches, while improving computational efficiency. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/alpayozkan/PixelwisePlanarity.

</details>


### [7] [Evaluation of MLLM-Agnostic Plug-and-Play Keyframe Selection Methods for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13250)
*Dilip Sarkar,Md. Safayet Islam,Liang Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对五种免训练即插即用（PaP）关键帧选择方法进行统一评测，使用3个MLLM在3个长视频理解基准上比较，发现QAaF在15个汇总设置中13个最优，FOCUS总体第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）受视觉token与算力预算限制，无法处理长视频的每一帧。现有提升长视频理解的三种途径中，重训练/扩展输入长度代价过高，训练适配器需大量训练资源，而免训练、即插即用（PaP）的MLLM无关适配器成本最低、最易普及。但已报道的五种PaP方法各自在不同基准、不同MLLM上评测，缺乏可比性。

Method: 统一评测五种免训练PaP关键帧选择方法：在3个MLLM与3个长视频理解基准上构建一致实验设置（共15个汇总评测情形），对比各方法的长视频理解表现。PaP方法可仅用候选帧，或结合候选帧与查询。

Result: 在15个汇总评测设置中，QAaF在13个设置上取得最佳性能；FOCUS总体排名第二。

Conclusion: 该工作为MLLM免训练关键帧选择方法提供了统一的实验参考基准，明确了各方法的相对优劣，便于后续研究在共同标准下比较与选择。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) cannot process every frame of a long video because of limitations in visual-token and computational budgets. Three primary approaches have been proposed to enhance their long-video understanding capabilities: (i) Retraining an MLLM on a large video corpus and/or extending its input length; (ii) Training an adapter for a specific MLLM that takes the entire video and the query as input and selects the most relevant video frames; and (iii) Developing a training-free, plug-and-play (PaP) adapter that is MLLM-agnostic. We refer to the third approach as PaP keyframe selection. A PaP method may use only candidate video frames without considering the query, or it may use both candidate video frames and the query. The first approach is prohibitively expensive. The second approach requires substantial training time and computational resources, but it is accessible to many because an adapter contains significantly fewer trainable parameters than an entire MLLM. The third approach has the lowest computational cost and is therefore broadly accessible. To the best of our knowledge, only five PaP methods have been reported within the past year. All of these methods have been evaluated on one or more video question-answering benchmarks and have demonstrated improvements in long-video understanding. However, the methods were evaluated on different benchmarks using different MLLMs. We present a comprehensive evaluation of these five methods using three MLLMs across three long-video understanding benchmarks. Our results show that QAaF achieves the best performance in 13 of the 15 aggregate evaluation settings, while FOCUS ranks second overall. These results provide a common experimental reference for comparing training-free keyframe-selection methods for MLLMs.

</details>


### [8] [Sampling headroom is not selection gain: a compute-value audit of test-time scaling for video world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13257)
*Yuhua Jiang,Junjie Lu,Feifei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Compute-Value Audit（CVA）序列化审计框架，用于检验测试时扩展（TTS）中"采样空间能否转化为可靠选择收益"，并在视频世界模型上给出系统性负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTS 只有在额外算力能产生更优候选、且系统能可靠识别它们时才能提升生成质量。视频世界模型中"更宽的采样池"未必改善最终被选中的输出，因此需要区分"采样空间（sampling headroom）"与"选择收益（selection gain）"。

Method: 提出 Compute-Value Audit（CVA）：一个四阶段序贯框架，依次检验 (1) 额外采样是否创造机会；(2) 可观测信号是否提供可靠状态；(3) 该状态是否支持有益动作；(4) 收益是否超过生成与验证的全部"入场费"。实验涵盖 Physics-IQ 192 个场景的池扩展（4→16）、Flow/Cycle/VideoReward 三种验证器、三种生成器上的 12 种自适应深度策略、VideoPhy2 上的 Predict-and-Perturb 干预（匹配 60-NFE、三个新种子重复），以及 PRM800K、MMLU-Pro 和特权配对未来（privileged paired future）的对照设置。

Result: 在 192 个 Physics-IQ 场景上，池从 4 扩到 16 使 oracle 质量提升 +9.23 IQ（95% CI [+7.44, +11.14]），但 Flow、Cycle、VideoReward 均无法可靠回收该空间；三种生成器下 12 种自适应深度策略无一优于均匀算力分配，仅回收测得入场费的 42–69%；VideoPhy2 的 Predict-and-Perturb 干预在三个重复中同样为负。负面结果并非普遍：anchor-explorer 在稀疏 PRM800K 设置中通过全部四阶段，MMLU-Pro 揭示"预测状态"与"有益动作"之间的落差，特权配对未来给出正向的视频上界。

Conclusion: 采样空间只有在能被转化为可靠决策、且其收益能承受完整算力开销时，才具备实际部署价值；单纯扩大采样池并不等于选择收益。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) can improve generation only when additional compute produces better candidates and the system can reliably identify them. This distinction is especially important for video world models, where a wider sample pool may contain stronger rollouts without improving the output that is ultimately selected. We introduce the Compute-Value Audit (CVA), a sequential framework that asks whether extra sampling creates opportunity, observable signals provide a reliable state, that state supports a beneficial action, and the resulting gain exceeds the full entry fee of generation and verification. On 192 Physics-IQ scenes, expanding the pool from 4 to 16 candidates increases oracle quality by +9.23 IQ (95% CI [+7.44, +11.14]), but Flow, Cycle, and VideoReward fail to recover this headroom reliably. Across three generators, none of twelve adaptive-depth policies outperforms uniform compute; they recover only 42-69% of the measured entry fee. A matched-60-NFE Predict-and-Perturb intervention on VideoPhy2 is likewise negative across three fresh-seed replicas. These negative results are not universal: anchor-explorer passes all four stages in a sparse PRM800K setting, MMLU-Pro exposes the gap between predictive state and useful action, and a privileged paired future establishes a positive video upper bound. Together, these results show that sampling headroom has deployment value only when it can be converted into a reliable decision whose benefit survives the complete compute charge. Code is available at https://github.com/YuhuaJiang2002/sampling-headroom-is-not-selection-gain.

</details>


### [9] [Filling the Unseen: Scene Extrapolation via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13262)
*Yunlai Zhou,Yiren Lu,Tuo Liang,Disheng Liu,Vipin Chaudhary,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的外推与插值框架，通过独立视角检测实现并行无冲突外推，配合分层流水线与即插即用QA-Mask，缓解生成-重建循环的误差累积并保护已重建良好区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅在训练视角分布内可实现逼真重建，但在分布外新视角上退化，表现为未观测区域空洞与可观测区域伪影。现有工作将该任务建模为外推与插值并借助生成模型解决，但外推尺度与质量受限；且其"生成-重建-平移"循环逐步构建场景，每步都以前一步结果为条件，导致误差累积。此外，先前方法忽略了生成图像与原始图像的不一致性，损害了原本重建良好的区域。

Method: 1）设计独立的相机视角检测机制，以支持并行、无冲突的外推，规避对易错循环的依赖；2）在此基础上构建分层流水线，分别对独立与依赖相机视角进行外推；3）提出即插即用的质量感知掩码（QA-Mask）模块，通过像素级渲染质量校准学习权重，实现对生成数据的选性利用，防止生成引入的退化。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架性能优越，且QA-Mask可泛化至多种生成式重建模型。

Conclusion: 该工作以整体化视角统一外推与插值任务，通过并行无冲突外推与分层处理避免误差累积，并以QA-Mask选择性融合生成数据保护良好重建区域，为分布外新视角合成提供了通用且可复用的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting achieves photorealistic reconstruction within training view distribution, yet it degrades on out-of-distribution novel views, exhibiting holes in unobserved regions and artifacts in observable areas. Recent works formulate this task as extrapolation and interpolation and try to address it with generative models, but remain limited in extrapolation scale and quality. They repeat a generate-reconstruct-shift cycle to progressively build a scene, which introduces accumulated errors with every step conditioning on previous outcomes. In this work, we propose a holistic framework for extrapolation and interpolation. We devise an independent camera view detection mechanism to enable parallel conflict-free extrapolation, circumventing the reliance on the aforementioned error-prone cycle. Building upon this, we design a hierarchical pipeline that extrapolates independent and dependent camera views separately. Additionally, previous methods overlook inconsistency between generated and original images, resulting in compromising well-reconstructed areas. We propose a plug-and-play Quality-Aware Mask (QA-Mask) module, enabling selective utilization of generated data. By calibrating learning weights with pixel-wise rendering quality, it prevents generation-induced degradations on well-constructed areas. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of our framework, with QA-Mask generalizing to multiple generative reconstruction models.

</details>


### [10] [Capability-Routed Visual Retrieval and Evidence Threading for Long-Context Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13268)
*Amirul Rahman,Aisha Karim,Kenji Nakamura,Yi-Fan Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在冻结的Qwen2.5-VL-7B生成器与ColPali/VisRAG-Ret页面索引之上，插入CAVR、WSPS、VET三个模块，提升复杂文档页面的检索与生成准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 年报、尽调包和信息图仪表盘将数字埋在页面图像中（坐标轴、单元格网格、脚注），OCR流水线会将其压平，页面级视觉检索器仍把上下文示例视为可互换，导致页面级视觉检索与生成效果受限。

Method: 保持生成器与页面索引冻结，仅插入三个模块：能力感知视觉路由器（CAVR）将检索页面标记为文本、表格、图表、布局或混合，并在生成前混合专家；弱到强页面选择（WSPS）将冻结的7B可回答性教师蒸馏为3B选择头，使排序不再依赖单一InfoNCE分数；视觉证据线索（VET）构建长度至多三的布局锚定路径，让生成器读取线索而非扁平的top-k列表。

Result: 在gold-page DocVQA/ChartQA/InfographicVQA上，7B系统达到96.3/90.1/85.4；VisRAG top-3协议下平均生成准确率62.74，优于同骨干拼接的59.39；MMLongBench-Doc retrieve-then-read的F1从19.2提升至22.6，多页准确率从16.4提升至21.2；WSPS重排后ViDoRe nDCG@5为83.6，TAT-DQA财务报告为70.4。

Conclusion: 通过能力感知路由、弱到强页面选择与布局锚定证据线索，可在不改动冻结生成器与索引的前提下，显著提升复杂文档页面级检索与多页生成性能。

Abstract: Annual reports, diligence packs, and infographic dashboards bury numbers in page images: axes, cell grids, and footnotes that OCR pipelines flatten and that page-level visual retrievers still treat as interchangeable in-context examples. We keep a frozen Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct generator and a ColPali / VisRAG-Ret page index, and insert three modules. A capability-aware visual router (CAVR) tags each retrieved page as text, table, chart, layout, or mixed and mixes specialist experts before generation. Weak-to-strong page selection (WSPS) distils a frozen 7B answerability teacher into a 3B selection head so ranking is no longer a single InfoNCE score. Visual evidence threading (VET) builds layout-anchored paths of length at most three and lets the generator read the thread rather than a flat top-$k$ list. On gold-page DocVQA / ChartQA / InfographicVQA the 7B system reaches 96.3 / 90.1 / 85.4. Under the VisRAG top-3 protocol the mean generation accuracy is 62.74 versus 59.39 for the same backbone with concatenation. On MMLongBench-Doc retrieve-then-read, F1 moves from 19.2 to 22.6 and multi-page accuracy from 16.4 to 21.2. ViDoRe nDCG@5 after WSPS reranking is 83.6, with TAT-DQA financial reports at 70.4.

</details>


### [11] [Frame-Synchronous Hand Gesture Detection by Projected Winding Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13269)
*Amey Thakur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文把"开掌绕长轴翻转"的同步问题转化为单一标量的过零点检测，证明该标量可分解为 k(θ)cos(θ)、|k|>0，从而无需分类器、训练数据和标定即可精确定位手势发生的瞬间，并给出手部交叉下仍正确的覆盖判定与基于噪声统计的参数推导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频手势识别通常被当作分类问题（逐帧打标签再据此执行动作），这对控制类应用尚可（命令晚几帧执行用户不易察觉），但对同步类应用不足——输出必须对齐到手势物理发生的具体帧。作者针对"开掌绕长轴旋转"这一常见动作研究同步问题。

Method: 定义 s 为腕部两条掌缘与外侧指关节在相机投影下二维叉积的归一化量，证明 s = k(θ)cos(θ) 且处处 |k|>0，故 s 恰在手掌侧对相机时为零、其符号指示朝向相机的掌面；由此把检测化为单标量过零（得到瞬时而非区间），并从代数形式证明该判据对镜像、手部尺度与左右手不变。双手交互部分以四个指尖为窗口观察重构场景，给出在双手交叉时仍正确的覆盖判定（常规三角剖分在此失效），并依据噪声统计而非人工观察推导三个风格化算子的各自由参数。

Result: 在具有精确真值的数据集上，该判据检出 95% 的翻转，在 240 段近失误序列中无假阳性，平均定位误差 6.7 ms，约为手部观测间隔（约 40 ms 的六分之一），优于报告包围样本的 23.0 ms。

Conclusion: 把手势视为连续量的零点而非离散标签，使事件报告可以比采样间隔更细，从而在无需分类器、训练数据和标定的情况下实现亚采样精度的同步。

Abstract: Gesture recognition on video is normally posed as classification: label each frame, then act on the label. That is adequate for control, where a command may be obeyed several frames late without a user noticing, and inadequate for synchronisation, where an output must be aligned to the frame on which the gesture physically occurred. We take the synchronisation problem for one common movement, the rotation of an open hand about its long axis, and show that it admits an exact solution needing no classifier, no training data and no calibration.
  Let s be the normalised two-dimensional cross product of the two palm edges at the wrist and the outer knuckles, under the projection the camera already performs. We prove that s factorises as k(theta)cos(theta) with |k| > 0 everywhere, so s vanishes exactly when the palm is edge-on and its sign tracks the face presented to the camera. Detection is therefore a zero crossing of one scalar, which yields an instant rather than an interval, and we prove the criterion invariant to image mirroring, hand scale and handedness, all from its algebraic form rather than from the landmark estimator.
  A two-handed interaction uses four fingertips as a window onto a restyled version of the same scene. We give a coverage predicate that stays correct when the hands cross, where the usual triangulation does not, and derive each free parameter of the three stylisation operators from a noise statistic rather than by inspection.
  Against a corpus with exact ground truth the criterion detects 95% of flips with no false positive in 240 near-miss sequences, and places each within 6.7 ms on average of the instant it occurred, a sixth of the interval at which the hand is observed, against 23.0 ms for reporting the bracketing sample. Reporting an event more finely than one samples follows from treating a gesture as the zero of a continuous quantity rather than a label.

</details>


### [12] [SomBench: Benchmark Dataset for Advancing Machine Learning in Lunar Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13277)
*Himanshu Patil,Gabby Nyirjesy,Rachel A. Slank,Vishal Gaur,Daniela Szwarcman,Paolo Fraccaro,Nikolaos Dionelis,Michael K. Barker,Andrew Annex,Vishnu Viswanathan,Zachary Morse,Ethan I. Schaefer,Hiyam Debary,Ankur Kumar,Rohit Lal,Geoffrey Dawson,Campbell Watson,Rebekah I. Dawson-Rigas,Manil Maskey,Juan Bernabé-Moreno,Rahul Ramachandran,Sujit Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SomBench——统一、空间对齐、ML就绪的月球数据集，整合4次任务10台仪器30+共配准图层，配套基准任务与基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球轨道任务（LRO、Kaguya/SELENE、GRAIL、Lunar Prospector等）积累了大量多仪器观测，但采样、投影与约定异构，阻碍可复现的机器学习研究。

Method: 构建SomBench：聚合4次任务、10台仪器、30余个共配准图层，分辨率1米至20千米/像素，覆盖纬度82°以内，采用90个月球横轴墨卡托分区加两个极区立体投影帽；图像锚定的分块流水线生成预训练就绪的多模态瓦片视图与防泄漏划分，以netCDF分发并附Parquet目录；建立覆盖撞击过程、火山历史与极区挥发分的应用基准套件。

Result: ResNet-50与SwinV2-B基线实验确认各基准任务均可从发布输入中学习，为后续模型开发提供参考基线。

Conclusion: SomBench为月球多模态机器学习提供了统一、可复现的数据与基准基础，基线结果确立参考点。

Abstract: Lunar orbital missions, such as Lunar Reconnaissance Orbiter, Kaguya/SELENE, Gravity Recovery and Interior Laboratory, and Lunar Prospector, among others, provide rich multi-instrument observations, but their heterogeneity in sampling, projection, and conventions limits reproducible machine learning (ML). We introduce SomBench, a unified, spatially-aligned, ML-ready lunar dataset aggregating 30+ co-registered layers from ten instruments across four missions, spanning 1 meter to 20 kilometer/pixel and covering 82 degree latitude in 90 Lunar Transverse Mercator zones with two polar stereographic caps. An image-anchored tiling pipeline yields pretraining-ready multimodal tile views with leakage-safe splits, distributed as netCDF with Parquet catalogs. An application benchmark suite spans impact processes, volcanic history, and polar volatiles. Baseline experiments with ResNet-50 and SwinV2-B models confirm that each benchmark task is learnable from the released inputs, establishing reference points for future model development.

</details>


### [13] [The MODA General Attribute Suite: A Four-Track Evaluation Benchmark for Fashion Attribute Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13279)
*Arkid Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发布 MODA General Attribute Suite，一个四轨时尚属性抽取基准，通过独立测试集、指标与评估协议解决现有评测混杂、不可见属性误判和词汇不匹配未度量的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时尚属性抽取评测存在三类缺陷：跨问题异质的图像类型汇报单一聚合指标；图像中不可见的属性被当作普通负样本评分；数据集间词汇不匹配的影响仅被承认而未被度量。

Method: 构建四轨基准（局部服装裁剪、商品目录图、感知适用性的全身照、商品文本），每轨拥有冻结测试集、输入契约、指标与泄漏单元且从不平均；协议要求标签盲预测、预测文件 SHA-256 承诺、fail-closed 评分器，以及在各轨自然泄漏单元上的 10,000 次配对聚类 bootstrap，晋级需每轨区间为正。

Result: 报告了基线结果、六项未带来提升的干预实验，并公开评分器、切分构建器、含失败运行的预测文件与哈希，以及四条轨道中三条的 MODA_NER(V) 模型检查点供复用。

Conclusion: 文本模型仅发布基准而不分发模型权重；论文同时说明了证据的局限性，强调以分轨严格协议替代跨问题聚合的平均指标。

Abstract: Fashion attribute extraction is evaluated inconsistently: results are reported as single aggregate numbers across image types that pose different problems, fields that are not visible in an image are scored as ordinary negatives, and the effect of vocabulary mismatch between datasets is acknowledged but not measured. We release the MODA General Attribute Suite, a four-track benchmark that keeps these problems separate by construction. Each track (localized garment crops, catalogue product images, applicability-aware full-body photographs, and product text) carries its own frozen test set, input contract, metric, and leakage unit, and the tracks are never averaged. The protocol requires label-blind prediction, SHA-256 commitment of prediction files before any label is opened, a fail-closed scorer, and a 10,000-sample paired cluster bootstrap at each track's natural leakage unit; promotion requires a positive interval on every track rather than a favourable mean. We release the scorers, the split builders, our own prediction files with their hashes including the runs we lose, and the MODA_NER(V) model checkpoints for three of the four tracks. The text model is not distributed; its benchmark is. We report baseline results, six interventions that did not improve them, and the limitations of the evidence.

</details>


### [14] [RxScribe Bench: A Multi-Axis Benchmark for Evaluating Vision-Language Models on Indian Outpatient Prescriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13280)
*Somil,Vijay Saini,Vidit Verma,Riya,Aastha Batta,Vibhuti Malhotra,Chayan Khetan,Piyush Mittal,Puneet Poonia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RxScribe Bench，一个面向手写处方数字化的视觉语言模型评测基准，按临床严重程度将性能拆解为四个轴（正确性、幻觉、参与度、鲁棒性），而非单一聚合分数，并发现前沿模型无单一赢家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处方转录错误具有异质性：模型“凭空编造药物”与“误读清晰剂量”带来的临床风险截然不同，但现有评测通常只报告一个混合的准确率数字，将两类错误等同看待，掩盖了不同失败模式的临床严重性差异。

Method: 构建 RxScribe Bench 基准：输入仅为处方图像与输出 schema，模型生成结构化记录，再与形状一致的人工标注真值逐字段比对，每个字段额外标注可见性与可读性。评测分解为四个轴——Correctness、Hallucination、Engagement、Robustness；其中 Robustness 轴在支撑样本低于最小证据阈值时拒绝给出硬字段结果。对真实处方进行多模型评测，每张图像独立冷启动多次运行。

Result: 在真实处方的独立冷启动运行中评测前沿视觉语言模型，发现没有任何单一模型能在全部四个轴上同时胜出。

Conclusion: 聚合式准确率掩盖了临床上本质不同的失败模式；按临床严重程度分解的多轴评测更能揭示模型间互补的优劣势，提示处方转录评测应放弃单一分数范式。

Abstract: Prescription transcription errors are not interchangeable. A model that fabricates a drug and a model that misreads a legible dose pose very different clinical risks, yet prescription-transcription accuracy is typically reported as a single blended figure that treats the two as equivalent. We introduce RxScribe Bench, a benchmark for evaluating vision-language models on handwritten prescription digitization that decomposes performance into four axes tied to clinical severity, rather than folding everything into a single aggregated score. Given only a prescription image and an output schema, a model produces a structured record, which is then compared field by field against a human-authored ground truth of identical shape, with each field also labeled for visibility and legibility. The four axes isolate distinct failure modes, namely Correctness, Hallucination, Engagement, and Robustness. The Robustness axis withholds its hard-field results rather than reporting one when the supporting sample falls below a minimum-evidence threshold. We evaluate frontier vision-language models on real prescriptions across independent cold runs per image, and find that no single model wins across all four axes.

</details>


### [15] [Global-Local Contextual Progressive Expansion Network for Martian Landslide Segmentation in Multimodal Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13332)
*Leo Thomas Ramos,Sidike Paheding,Abel A. Reyes-Angulo,Rajaneesh A.,Sajinkumar K. S.,Angel D. Sappa,Thomas Oommen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估深度学习方法用于火星滑坡分割，并提出TransCPLES网络，在七波段多模态数据集MMLSv2上取得最优综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星滑坡分割对理解地表过程与未来空间探索具有重要意义，但该任务研究不足：滑坡形态多变、前景区域稀疏且不规则、轨道观测的光谱与地形信息异质融合，均构成开放挑战。

Method: 对当代主流神经分割模型进行广泛评估；提出TransCPLES——一种U形网络，将上下文渐进层扩展（Contextual Progressive Layer Expansion）特征提取与基于Transformer的上下文推理相结合，以同时捕捉局部地貌模式与更大范围的空间依赖。

Result: 在七波段多模态火星滑坡数据集MMLSv2上，于地理上分布不同的样本评估中取得最佳综合性能；在不同滑坡规模下保持一致的边界勾画、稳定的前景判别，并在精度与计算开销之间取得良好平衡，优于多种卷积、注意力及Transformer分割模型。

Conclusion: 该工作是该领域首个全面探索，为行星遥感中的深度学习提供参考，并鼓励后续研究；代码将在发表后公开。

Abstract: Automated landslide segmentation on Mars is one of the important tasks for understanding its surface processes, and all will aid in future space exploration. However, it remains a relatively underexplored open challenge because landslide morphology is highly variable, foreground regions are often sparse or irregular, and orbital observations combine heterogeneous spectral and topographic cues. In this context, this work investigates the capability of deep learning to address Martian landslide segmentation through an extensive assessment of modern neural segmentation models. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to provide such a comprehensive exploration in this domain. We further propose TransCPLES, a U-shaped network that couples Contextual Progressive Layer Expansion feature extraction with Transformer-based contextual reasoning, enabling the model to capture local geomorphic patterns and broader spatial dependencies for more reliable landslide delineation. Experiments on MMLSv2, a seven-band multimodal Martian landslide dataset, show that TransCPLES achieves the best overall performance when evaluated on geographically distinct samples, with consistent delineation across different landslide extents, stable foreground discrimination, and a favorable balance between accuracy and computational cost compared with several state-of-the-art convolutional, attention-based, and Transformer-based segmentation models. With this work, we hope to provide a useful reference and encourage further research and development in deep learning for planetary remote sensing. Code will be available after publication.

</details>


### [16] [Rank-Consistent Set Reasoning for Co-Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13706)
*Yuan Xiang,Matteo Rossi,Yingzhou Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Rank-Consistent Set Reasoning (RCSR)，把共显著目标检测中的图像组建模为无序集合，通过多尺度组槽排序与秩一致性门控、鲁棒截尾统计聚合进行稠密预测，无需语言分支、开放词汇检测器或外部分割模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共显著目标检测需同时识别单图显著且被整组支持的 foreground 区域，但以序列或语义标签方式建模图像组易受偶然成对匹配和个别异常样本主导，缺乏集合级性质约束。

Method: RCSR 为监督式稠密预测框架：集合编码器由多尺度视觉特征构建组槽；在每个图像尺度上对空间区域与组槽的一致性排序，并用鲁棒截尾统计聚合；秩一致性门控衡量候选区域排序在组内成员间的稳定性；门控槽与逐图特征联合解码生成共显著图。训练引入组排列目标与硬干扰物增强，避免记忆图像顺序或孤立视觉显著性。

Result: 在 CoCA、CoSal2015、CoSOD3k 上建立评估协议，并进行组大小鲁棒性、干扰物抑制、顺序不变性与跨数据集迁移测试。

Conclusion: 将图像组视为无序集合并以秩一致集合推理建模，可抑制偶然成对匹配和异常样本主导，学习集合级目标性质；模型不依赖自然语言分支、开放词汇检测器和外部分割模型。

Abstract: Co-salient object detection (Co-SOD) requires a model to find foreground regions that are salient in individual images and supported by the image group. We present \emph{Rank-Consistent Set Reasoning} (RCSR), a supervised dense-prediction framework that models a group as an unordered set rather than as a sequence of images or a semantic label. The core idea is to rank how strongly each spatial region agrees with a small collection of learned group slots at every image scale, and to aggregate these ranks with a robust trimmed statistic. This suppresses accidental pairwise matches and prevents one atypical group member from dominating the shared representation. A set encoder builds group slots directly from multi-scale visual features, while a rank-consistency gate measures whether the ordering of candidate regions is stable across group members. The gated slots are decoded jointly with per-image features to produce co-saliency maps. The model contains no natural-language branch, no open-vocabulary detector, and no external segmentation model. We further introduce a group permutation objective and hard-distractor augmentation so that the model learns the properties of a set-level target rather than memorizing image order or isolated visual saliency. We formulate an evaluation protocol for CoCA, CoSal2015, and CoSOD3k, together with tests of group-size robustness, distractor rejection, order invariance, and cross-dataset transfer.

</details>


### [17] [Hyper-LLaVA: Hyperbolic Uncertainty-aware Modality-Balanced Routing for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13742)
*Kunlun Xu,Yanqin Zhang,Wenwen Qiang,Jiahuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hyper-LLaVA，通过双曲空间中的样本-任务分布相似度建模与跨模态不确定性自适应平衡，改进多模态持续指令微调的参数路由能力，性能显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态持续指令微调（MCIT）的参数路由方法依赖样本到任务中心的相似度及等权跨模态融合，存在两个根本缺陷：(1) 模态内仅用样本-任务中心距离进行路由，未充分利用任务内丰富的多样性信息，匹配次优；(2) 不同模态在各任务上的可靠性不同，存在任务间歧义的模态易误导路由结果。

Method: 提出Hyper-LLaVA（双曲不确定性感知的模态平衡路由）。针对模态内任务匹配，在双曲空间中度量样本到任务的分布相似度；针对不可靠模态导致的性能退化，量化每个模态内的任务匹配歧义度，实现跨模态任务匹配的自适应平衡。二者互补，共同强化模态内与模态间的任务匹配。

Result: 基于模态内与模态间任务匹配的互补增强，Hyper-LLaVA以较大幅度超越当前最先进方法。源码地址：https://github.com/zhoujiahuan1991/ICML2026-Hyper-LLaVA

Conclusion: 通过双曲空间分布相似度建模与不确定性驱动的模态自适应平衡，可有效提升MCIT参数路由的容量与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) aims to exploit the incrementally accumulated knowledge to process multimodal inputs of diverse tasks, where parameter routing plays an important role. State-of-the-art methods rely on sample-to-task center similarity and cross-modal fusion with equal weight during routing. However, such solutions face two fundamental flaws: (1) Within each modality, the sample-to-task center distance is sub-optimal for routing since the abundant intra-task diversity information is underleveraged. (2) Different modalities exhibit varying reliability across tasks, where the modality with inter-task ambiguity can easily misguide the routing result. To address these problems, we propose Hyperbolic Uncertainty-aware Modality-Balanced Routing (Hyper-LLaVA) to improve parameter routing capacity based on cross-modality task feature uncertainty modeling. Specifically, to improve intra-modality task matching, Hyper-LLaVA accesses the sample-to-task distribution similarity in the Hyperbolic space. Besides, to alleviate the degradation brought by unreliable modalities, Hyper-LLaVA quantifies the task matching ambiguity within each modality to achieve adaptive balancing between task matching across modalities. Based on the complementary intra- and inter-modality task matching enhancement, our Hyper-LLaVA outperforms state-of-the-art approaches by large margins. Our source code is available at https://github.com/zhoujiahuan1991/ICML2026-Hyper-LLaVA

</details>


### [18] [Event-Level Emotion Recognition in the Wild Using Deep Facial Expression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13854)
*Aleksandr Semerikov,Pakizar Shamoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个端到端的、从照片进行事件级情绪识别的流水线：先检测人脸、用CNN分类表情、再聚合人脸级情绪概率以估计公共事件整体情绪分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景下的面部情绪识别（FER）受多张人脸、遮挡、姿态变化与复杂光照影响而困难；现有研究多聚焦个体表情分类，未涉及事件层面的集体情绪状态分析。

Method: 提出端到端流水线：对每张图像进行人脸检测，使用深度卷积神经网络分类面部表情，并将人脸级情绪概率聚合为事件整体情绪分布。并在FER-2013与RAF-DB数据集上比较多种CNN架构。

Result: 在FER-2013与RAF-DB上的对比评估表明，基于RAF-DB训练的EfficientNet-B2迁移学习更适合真实RGB数据；在包含1658张图像的真实事件数据集上评估，结果显示事件子集间情绪分布稳定。

Conclusion: 事件级聚合方法对野外（in the wild）情绪分析有效，验证了从个体表情到集体情绪的事件级分析可行性。

Abstract: Facial emotion recognition (FER) in real-world environments remains challenging due to unconstrained imaging conditions, including multiple faces, occlusions, pose variations, and complex lighting. Most existing studies focus on individual facial emotion classification and do not address the analysis of collective emotional states at the event level. This paper proposes an end-to-end pipeline for event-level emotion recognition from photographs. The approach detects faces in each image, classifies facial expressions using a deep convolutional neural network, and aggregates face-level emotion probabilities to estimate the overall emotional distribution of a public event. A comparative evaluation of several CNN architectures on the FER- 2013 and RAF-DB datasets demonstrates that transfer learning with EfficientNet-B2 trained on RAF-DB is more suitable for real-world RGB data. The proposed method is evaluated on a real-world event dataset containing 1658 images. Experimental results show stable emotion distributions across event subsets, confirming the effectiveness of event-level aggregation for emotion analysis in the wild.

</details>


### [19] [SkyAnchor: Updating Metric-scale Aerial 3D Gaussian Scenes from Unposed Ground-View Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13903)
*Zhuoxiao Li,Xinyi Liu,Taoyu Wu,Yinrui Ren,Tongyan Hua,Ou Jing,Shuai Zhang,Dongli Wu,Rongjun Qin,Ge Lin Kan,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SkyAnchor，以既有航拍SfM与3DGS场景为固定支架，将无位姿、无尺度的地面视频序列配准并融合，实现街景级外观更新，同时保持航拍视角质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有航拍场景具备可靠度量SfM与预训练3DGS模型，但缺少街景细节；新采集的地面序列虽可补充街景外观，却无相机位姿与全局尺度。直接配准的两大难点是：单图像跨视角定位脆弱，长轨迹增量注册易漂移。因此需避免从零联合重建航拍与地面影像。

Method: 将航拍场景视为固定支架：先对连续地面帧的短片段在几何验证的航拍支撑下定位，生成稀疏锚点位姿；再用锚点约束的子图恢复完整地面轨迹，在增量注册与光束法平差中固定前后锚点位姿；最后插入经筛选的地面高斯并保留航拍视角，辅以轻量联合优化。

Result: 在七个真实航拍—地面场景上验证，获得准确的度量地面轨迹，并生成更新后的3D高斯场景，在航拍与地面视角下均具较强渲染质量。

Conclusion: 以航拍场景为锚定支架的分阶段配准与高斯插入策略，可有效解决无位姿地面序列的尺度与漂移问题，实现航拍-地面一致的场景更新，为跨视角场景扩展提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We study how to update a pre-built aerial scene with a newly captured, unposed ground-view sequence. The aerial scene already contains a reliable metric Structure-from-Motion (SfM) reconstruction and a pre-trained 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model, whereas the ground-view sequence is collected later to add street-level appearance but has unknown camera poses and global scale. Registering this sequence to the aerial SfM reconstruction is challenging because single-image cross-view localization is brittle and long trajectories are prone to drift. To address these challenges, we present SkyAnchor, which treats the existing aerial scene as a fixed scaffold for ground-view registration and scene update instead of jointly reconstructing aerial and ground imagery from scratch. It first localizes short groups of consecutive ground frames against geometrically verified aerial support, producing sparse anchor poses. It then recovers the full ground trajectory with anchor-constrained submaps, fixing the front and rear anchor poses during incremental registration and bundle adjustment. Finally, it inserts filtered ground Gaussians while preserving the aerial view, followed by lightweight joint refinement. Experiments on seven real aerial--ground scenes show accurate metric ground trajectories and updated 3D Gaussian scenes with strong aerial- and ground-view rendering quality.

</details>


### [20] [SGWIB:Sliced Gromov-Wasserstein Information Bottleneck for Video Highlight Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13966)
*Hanjuan Huang,Yung-Chieh Yeh,Hsing-Kuo Pao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结构感知的信息瓶颈框架SGWIB用于视频高光检测，通过Sliced Gromov-Monge Gap正则项保留片段间时序结构，并结合上下文伪标签与解耦模块减少运动场景的上下文偏差，在两数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频高光检测需要既能判别片段表示，又能保留近邻与远距离片段间的时序关系。信息瓶颈原理虽能学习紧凑且任务相关的表示，但尚未用于该任务，且直接套用传统形式会忽略片段间关系结构，并在压缩过程中破坏与高光相关的时序组织。

Method: 提出Sliced Gromov-Monge Gap（SGMG），一种结构感知正则项，度量给定源到瓶颈映射相对最优切片结构对应所产生的多余关系畸变；基于SGMG构建SGWIB信息瓶颈框架，学习紧凑瓶颈表示的同时保留片段间时序结构。此外引入Home-Away-Related Contextual Pseudo-Labels及上下文解耦模块，通过分离高光导向信息与上下文模式来降低运动场景特有的上下文偏差。

Result: 在MrHiSum和MoSu数据集上，SGWIB在Kendall's tau、Spearman's rho、mAP@50、mAP@30四项指标上均优于所比较的单模态方法。在MrHiSum上，视觉模型相较此前最优结果分别提升0.031、0.031、0.87和0.75。

Conclusion: 结构感知的信息瓶颈正则化结合上下文解耦能够有效提升片段级高光预测性能。

Abstract: Video highlight detection aims to identify temporally important segments that capture the most informative or engaging events in a video. Reliable prediction therefore requires not only discriminative segment representations but also preservation of the temporal relationships among neighboring and distant segments. The information bottleneck principle has proven effective for learning compact and task-relevant representations, yet it has not been explored for video highlight detection, and applying conventional formulations directly would overlook inter-segment relational structure and distort highlight relevant temporal organization during compression. We therefore introduce the Sliced Gromov-Monge Gap (SGMG), a structure aware regularizer that measures the excess relational distortion induced by a prescribed source-to-bottleneck mapping relative to an optimal sliced structural correspondence. Building on SGMG, we develop SGWIB, an information-bottleneck framework for single-modal video highlight detection that learns compact bottleneck representations while preserving inter-segment temporal structure. We further introduce Home-Away-Related Contextual Pseudo-Labels and a contextual disentanglement module that reduce sports-specific contextual bias by separating highlight oriented information from contextual patterns. Experiments on MrHiSum and MoSu show that SGWIB attains the best Kendall's tau, Spearman's rho, mAP@50, and mAP@30 among the compared single-modal methods on both datasets. On MrHiSum, the visual model improves the strongest previous results by 0.031, 0.031, 0.87, and 0.75 on these four metrics, respectively. These results show that structure-aware information-bottleneck regularization combined with contextual disentanglement improves segment-level highlight prediction.

</details>


### [21] [Quantum-Gated LiteSSD: A Parameter-Efficient Lightweight Hybrid Quantum-Classical Framework for Forward-Looking Sonar Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14025)
*Niloy Kumar Mondal,Poulomi Sarker Puja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Quantum-Gated LiteSSD，将 QuCNet 式多电路量子处理重新表述为以恒等为中心的信道门控机制，用于前视声呐目标检测，在嵌入式平台上实现了参数高效与精度之间的良好折衷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前视声呐目标检测对水下感知至关重要，但在嵌入式平台上部署需要高度紧凑的模型，现有检测器参数规模过大，难以满足部署需求。

Method: 探索量子计算，提出混合量子-经典检测器 Quantum-Gated LiteSSD，将 QuCNet 风格的多电路量子处理重构为以恒等为中心的信道门控机制，用于空间特征调制。

Result: 在 Marine Debris Watertank 数据集上达到 90.84% mAP50，参数比 YOLO26s 少约 62 倍、比 SSD-VGG16 少约 164.3 倍；在 UATD 上以仅 0.150M 参数达到 70.37% mAP50，比 SSGA-YOLO 小约 4.1 倍，并保持有效的多类检测能力。

Conclusion: 该方法在参数-精度权衡上表现有效，验证了量子门控机制在前视声呐嵌入式目标检测中的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Forward-looking sonar object detection is essential for underwater perception, yet deployment on embedded platforms requires highly compact models. To address this challenge, we explore quantum computing and introduce Quantum-Gated LiteSSD, a parameter-efficient hybrid quantum--classical detector that reformulates QuCNet-style multi-circuit quantum processing as an identity-centered channel-gating mechanism for spatial feature modulation. Experiments on the Marine Debris Watertank dataset and UATD forward-looking sonar benchmarks demonstrate an effective parameter--accuracy trade-off. The proposed detector achieves 90.84% $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ on Watertank with approximately $62\times$ fewer parameters than YOLO26s and $164.3\times$ fewer parameters than SSD-VGG16. On UATD, the model achieves 70.37% $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ with only 0.150M parameters, making it approximately $4.1\times$ smaller than SSGA-YOLO while retaining meaningful multi-class detection capability.

</details>


### [22] [Bridging the Synthetic-to-Real Gap for Few-Shot Cryo-ET Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14097)
*Siddhant Bharadwaj,Ashish Vashist,Rashi Singh,Pranav Vinodh,Nishanth Artham,Runmin Jiang,Xingjian Li,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可学习变换模块的合成到真实域适应框架，在输入与特征层面弥合冷冻电子断层扫描子断层图分类的域差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷冻电子断层扫描（cryo-ET）中的子断层图分类因标注样本稀缺而困难。虽然可用cryo-ET模拟器生成无限合成数据，但合成与真实子断层图间存在显著域差异，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出一种新颖的合成到真实域适应框架，包含一个可学习变换模块，在输入层面和特征层面同时弥合合成与真实数据间的域差异。

Result: 大量实验表明，在少样本设置下，本方法持续优于现有迁移学习基线方法。

Conclusion: 该合成到真实域适应框架能有效利用合成数据缓解标注稀缺问题，在少样本cryo-ET子断层图分类任务中取得优越性能。

Abstract: Subtomogram classification in cryo-electron tomography (cryo-ET) is a challenging problem due to the scarcity of labeled examples. While cryo-ET simulators can be adopted to generate unlimited synthetic data, the substantial domain gap between synthetic and real subtomograms hinders its practical utilization. In this work, we propose a novel synthetic-to-real adaptation framework with a learnable transformation module, bridging this gap at both the input and feature levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing transfer learning baselines in few-shot settings.

</details>


### [23] [RA-CoA: Training-free Fashion Image Captioning via Retrieval-Augmented Chain-of-Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14100)
*Abhirama Subramanyam Penamakuri,Shreya Shukla,Anand Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RA-CoA，一种免训练、模型无关的检索增强属性链框架，将时尚图像描述拆解为属性集检索与属性级推理两阶段，在冻结的视觉语言模型上平均提升 METEOR 26.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚图像描述（FIC）需要细粒度视觉推理与领域专业术语知识（如领口、闭合方式、图案、裙型等），且时尚库存随潮流与词汇快速变化，因此需要可扩展、免训练的方案。指令微调的通用视觉语言模型虽有强零样本能力与语言流畅度，但属性覆盖与精度不足，易对细粒度时尚细节产生幻觉或误识别，难以满足商品编目、个性化推荐等高保真应用需求。

Method: RA-CoA（Retrieval-Augmented Chain-of-Attributes）是一个免训练、模型无关的框架，将时尚图像描述解耦为两个可解释阶段：(i) 从商品知识库中检索相关属性集；(ii) 进行属性级推理以生成最终描述。该方法与冻结的视觉语言模型配合使用，无需微调即可提升商品描述中的细粒度属性精度。

Result: 在多种视觉语言模型族与不同提示范式下进行了广泛评估，RA-CoA 显著提升描述质量，相较零样本描述平均获得 26.3% 的 METEOR 分数提升。代码已公开。

Conclusion: RA-CoA 通过检索增强与属性链推理，在不微调的前提下有效缓解通用视觉语言模型在时尚领域的细粒度属性幻觉与误识别问题，提升时尚图像描述精度，具备良好的可扩展性与实际适配性。

Abstract: Fashion Image Captioning (FIC) plays a vital role in enhancing user experience and product search in e-commerce platforms. Unlike natural scene image captioning, FIC requires fine-grained visual reasoning and knowledge of domain-specific terminology to capture subtle attributes such as neckline and closure types, graphic patterns, and dress silhouettes. Moreover, as fashion inventories evolve rapidly with new trends, styles, and frequently emerging vocabulary, developing training-free captioning solution becomes essential for scalability and real-world adaptability. Instruction-tuned vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promising solution to fashion image captioning dueto their strong zero-shot capabilities and natural language fluency. However, these general-purpose models often lack attribute-level coverage and precision, and tend to hallucinate or misidentify fine-grained fashion details, making them less suitable for high-fidelity applications like product cataloging or personalized recommendations. To address this, we propose RA-CoA (Retrieval-Augmented Chain-of-Attributes), a novel, training-free framework that disentangles fashion image captioning into two interpretable stages: (i) retrieval of relevant attribute sets from a product knowledge base, and (ii) attribute-level reasoning to generate the final caption. RA-CoA is a model-agnostic approach that works with frozen VLMs to improve fine-grained attribute precision in product captions without the need for fine-tuning. Extensive evaluations across diverse VLM model families under different prompting paradigms demonstrate that RA-CoA significantly improves caption quality, achieving an average gain of 26.3% METEOR score over zero-shot captioning. We make our code publicly available.

</details>


### [24] [A Voxel-Spacing-Aware Extension of PyRadiomics for Anisotropic Texture Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14103)
*David Corral Fontecha,Juan Miranda Bautista,Pablo Menendez Fernández-Miranda,Andrea Trapote Fernandez,Lara Lloret Iglesias,Jose A. Vega*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究报告了一种体素间距感知的PyRadiomics扩展，在不进行灰度插值的前提下，通过各向异性相对的特征计算方式，消除各向异性CT/MRI中固定体素偏移对应不同物理距离所带来的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性CT和MRI中相同体素偏移代表不同物理距离，常规放射组学纹理特征提取会因此产生偏差；现有做法依赖插值重采样，可能引入伪影或改变灰度分布，因此需要一种无需插值的间距感知方案。

Method: 在PyRadiomics的Python前端、C封装与计算后端实现间距感知扩展：GLCM采用各向异性相对的"特征级"角度聚合；NGTDM采用各向异性相对的加权邻域平均；GLRLM、GLDM、GLSZM基于原生各向异性网格构建有限体积零阶保持（finite-volume zero-order-hold）表示；使用合成3D体模进行软件验证。

Result: 关闭间距感知模式时，修改版与标准PyRadiomics完全一致；各向同性间距下，75个纹理特征全部等价。各向异性间距下，方法选择性改变部分纹理族，且数值上区别于最近邻、线性与B样条重采样。性能分析显示运行时间与内存有适度增加；敏感性分析量化了有限体积取整效应，并确认间距感知差异在不同binWidth设置下持续存在。

Conclusion: 该框架为后续在异构医学影像数据集上评估间距感知放射组学提供了向后兼容的技术基础。

Abstract: Radiomic texture features are commonly extracted from anisotropic CT and MRI acquisitions, where identical voxel offsets may represent different physical distances. We implemented and validated a voxel-spacing-aware extension of PyRadiomics that incorporates spacing information without generating interpolated gray levels. The framework operates across the Python frontend, C wrapper, and computational backend. GLCM uses anisotropy-relative feature-level angular aggregation, NGTDM uses anisotropy-relative weighted neighborhood averaging, and GLRLM, GLDM, and GLSZM are computed on a finite-volume zero-order-hold representation derived from the native anisotropic grid. Synthetic 3D phantoms were used for software validation. The modified implementation reproduced standard PyRadiomics exactly when spacing-aware mode was disabled and remained equivalent under isotropic spacing across 75 texture features. Under anisotropic spacing, the method selectively modified texture families and was numerically distinct from nearest-neighbor, linear, and B-spline resampling. Computational profiling showed moderate runtime and memory increases, while sensitivity analyses quantified finite-volume rounding effects and confirmed that spacing-aware differences persisted across binWidth settings. The framework provides a backward-compatible technical basis for future evaluation of spacing-aware radiomics in heterogeneous medical imaging datasets.

</details>


### [25] [Accelerating HKTex without Mesh Eigensystems: Local Unfolding and Randomized Thermal Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14105)
*Zhewen He,Junyi Hu,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何消除Heat Kernel Textures (HKTex)在全局Laplace-Beltrami特征分解和固定基上的计算瓶颈，提出两条互补路线：LocalHK（局部核展开+解析log-map核）与ThermalRF（GPU稀疏Chebyshev+随机范围查找构建全局低秩热因子，并混合四个邻域热响应）。在保持训练器、密度控制与合成不变的前提下，两条路线分别大幅降低初始化/训练开销，同时维持或提升视图PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HKTex用内禀各向异性核表示表面外观，但依赖50个全局Laplace-Beltrami特征分解和常驻[50,V,256]基，存在显著的计算与初始化瓶颈。

Method: 提出两种互补方案：(1) LocalHK利用训练核的局部性，用半径受限的hinge展开和解析log-map核替代谱评估；(2) ThermalRF保留离散各向异性热半群，用GPU稀疏Chebyshev作用与随机范围查找构建全局低秩热因子（无需mesh规模特征向量），并用编译求值器混合四个邻域热响应。二者均不改动GeodesicOpt、密度控制和合成。

Result: LocalHK在10个Objaverse网格上平均视角PSNR从31.35→32.00 dB，8网格低多边形留出集从29.82→30.76 dB，初始化减少40.5倍，并支持749,570顶点代理运行（谱基线在此失败）。ThermalRF在spot和thin-stem挑战上端到端预处理、初始化与5,000步优化分别减少29.3%和24.4%，所有表面/图集/视角PSNR变化在0.12 dB内，训练分配减少约90%。

Conclusion: 两条路线揭示了一个关键设计权衡：最大局部性与尺度 vs 对热PDE的保真度。更广泛的热特征评估和真实大场景仍属未来工作。

Abstract: Heat Kernel Textures (HKTex) represent surface appearance with intrinsic anisotropic
  kernels, but evaluate them using 50 global Laplace-Beltrami eigendecompositions and a
  resident basis of shape [50,V,256]. We study two complementary ways to remove this
  bottleneck while leaving the trainer, GeodesicOpt, density control, and compositing
  unchanged. LocalHK exploits the measured locality of trained kernels and replaces
  spectral evaluation by radius-bounded hinge unfolding and an analytic log-map kernel.
  On 10 Objaverse meshes and an 8-mesh low-poly holdout, it changes mean view PSNR from
  31.35 to 32.00 and from 29.82 to 30.76 dB, respectively, while reducing
  initialization by 40.5 times and enabling a 749,570-vertex proxy-backed run where the
  spectral baseline fails. ThermalRF instead preserves the discrete anisotropic heat
  semigroup: GPU sparse Chebyshev actions and randomized range finding construct global
  low-rank heat factors without mesh-sized eigenvectors, and a compiled evaluator mixes
  four neighboring thermal responses. On spot and a thin-stem challenge, ThermalRF
  reduces end-to-end preprocessing, initialization, and 5,000-step optimization by
  29.3% and 24.4%, with every surface, atlas, or view PSNR change within 0.12 dB and
  training allocation reduced by about 90%. The two routes expose a useful design
  choice: maximal locality and scale versus fidelity to the thermal PDE. Broader
  thermal-feature evaluation and real large scenes remain future work.

</details>


### [26] [Talking to Me or Someone Else? Rethinking Talk-to-Me Detection in Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14118)
*Feiyu Du,Xi He,Jia Li,Yapeng Tian,Weili Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将"是否在与佩戴者对话"(TTM)问题从离线片段级二分类重构为在线帧级预测任务，引入多种非TTM说话状态，构建包含406个视频、约90万标注帧的Online TTM数据集，并提出融合音频、视觉与语音语义线索的多模态模型，在TTM上取得75.5%帧级F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTM研究通常被表述为离线片段级识别，与在线交互场景对齐较差，且忽视了自我中心视频中自然出现的多样化非TTM说话状态（如与他人交谈、自言自语、背景状态）。

Method: 将任务重构为在线帧级预测；不再把TTM视为对单一负类的二分类，而是在多样化非TTM状态下建模；扩展Ego4D社交交互基准，构建Online TTM Dataset（406个自我中心视频片段，约90万帧级标注，含背景、TTM、与他人交谈、自言自语等类别）；评估五种适配基线，并提出整合跨模态社交线索的新模型，联合利用音频、视觉与语音语义线索。

Result: 所提多模态模型在TTM上达到75.5%帧级F1，优于强基线，并支持系统分析不同说话状态对TTM识别的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了在线、帧级、多状态建模的TTM新范式，并提供数据集与多模态方法，为该方向的进一步研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Online understanding of who is talking to the camera wearer is a key capability for egocentric social interaction. However, existing talk-to-me (TTM) studies are commonly formulated as offline clip-level recognition, which is poorly aligned with online interaction and overlooks the diverse non-TTM speaking states that naturally arise in egocentric videos. In this paper, we revisit this problem by reformulating it as an online, frame-level prediction task. Instead of treating TTM as a binary problem against a single negative class, we model it in the presence of diverse and previously underexplored non-TTM states, such as talking-to-others, self-talking, and background conditions. To support this new formulation, we construct an Online TTM Dataset consisting of 406 egocentric video clips with approximately 900K annotated frames, each labeled with frame-level social interaction categories (e.g., background, TTM, talking-to-others, self-talking), by extending the Ego4D social interaction benchmark. In this benchmark, we evaluate five adapted baselines and develop a new model that integrates social cues across modalities. Experimental results show that our multimodal model, which jointly leverages audio, visual, and speech-semantic cues, achieves 75.5% frame-level F1 on TTM, outperforming strong baselines and enabling a systematic analysis of how different speaking states affect TTM recognition.

</details>


### [27] [SignMimic: Robust High-Quality Sign Language Motion Generation via Human-Shape-Oblivious Pose Transfer Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14122)
*Zhewen He,Junyi Yu,Haomian Huang,Zhenhua Li,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SignMimic 通过将手势视频模仿任务分解为刚性规范化、非刚性适配和补全三阶段，结合扩散模型实现身份与语言学形式保持的高质量手势视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有手势视频模仿方法将刚性运动、非刚性形变和视角相关补全耦合在单一生成器中，导致手型漂移和时空不稳定，需要解耦处理以注入几何与语言学先验。

Method: 提出 SignMimic，包含三步：(i) 用 TNet 类模型学习 SE(3) 刚性规范化以稳定全局姿态；(ii) 在规范空间中进行非刚性适配，通过 NIF2D 保留手/脸等细粒度关节与协同发音；(iii) 在条件视频扩散前使用 Pose-MAE 风格补全，实现几何与语言学先验的分解注入。

Result: 在 ASL 50K、How2Sign、CSL News 等大规模数据集上，SignMimic 在视频质量、身份相似度和帧连续性上达到 SOTA 级性能，同时在生成视频回译（SLT）中损失最小；消融实验验证了刚性规范化、非刚性适配与补全三部分的作用。

Conclusion: 分解式建模有效提升手势视频模仿的形状与时空一致性，代码、模型权重及视频示例将公开。

Abstract: We study the challenge of sign language video mimicking: given a driving video and a single reference frame, synthesize a video where the target signer reproduces the source
  motion while preserving identity and linguistic form. Prior pipelines entangle rigid motion, non-rigid deformation, and view-dependent completion in a monolithic generator,
  causing handshape drift and spatio-temporal instability. We present SignMimic, which (i) applies a TNet-based model to study SE(3) rigid canonicalization to stabilize global
  pose, (ii) performs non-rigid adaptation in a canonical space to preserve fine-grained articulators (hands/face) and coarticulation via NIF2D, and (iii) uses Pose-MAE-style
  completion before conditional video diffusion. This factorization injects geometric and linguistic priors, yielding shape and spatio-temporal consistency. On several large-scale
  datasets (ASL 50K, How2Sign, CSL News), SignMimic achieves state-of-the-art-level performance on video quality, identity similarity, and frame continuity while also achieving
  minimal loss when performing back translation (SLT) on generated videos. Ablations confirm the role of rigid canonicalization, non-rigid adaptation, and completion. Code, model
  checkpoints, and video examples will be released.

</details>


### [28] [3D Gait-Based Autism Classification Using Attention-Enhanced Deep Learning with Cross-Fold Statistical Stability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14159)
*Md Nadim Mahamood,Md Arif Shahriar,Md Parvej Sikder,Md Rasul Islam,Md Shafi Ud Doula,Md Ashraful Alam,Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种注意力增强的Transformer框架用于自闭症谱系障碍(ASD)步态自动分类，在公开Kinect数据集上五折交叉验证达到99.00%准确率，在独立私有测力台数据集上平均准确率达95.00%，并首次正式评估折间稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD早期诊断依赖传统临床评估，存在主观性强、耗时长、需专家参与等问题。步态作为非侵入性行为生物标志物具有自动化筛查潜力，但现有研究主要局限于单数据集评估、卷积架构，且仅对交叉验证性能做描述性总结，未正式评估折间稳定性。

Method: 提出注意力增强的Transformer分类框架，并在两种结构不同的3D步态特征表示上进行评估：预计算的统计步态描述符与原始生物力学地面反作用力测量。采用五折交叉验证，并正式评估折间稳定性，同时在公开基准与独立私有数据集上进行验证。

Result: 公开Kinect基准数据集上：准确率99.00%、精确率99.02%、召回率99.00%、F1分数99.00%、特异度99.00%，优于所对比的现有最先进方法。独立私有测力台数据集上均值：准确率95.00%、精确率93.81%、召回率96.67%、F1分数95.13%、特异度93.33%。

Conclusion: 注意力增强Transformer框架在两类异构3D步态特征表示上均表现出优异的ASD分类性能，且具备跨数据集泛化能力，验证了步态作为非侵入性ASD筛查生物标志物的有效性，并弥补了既有研究在架构选择、多数据集验证及折间稳定性评估方面的不足。

Abstract: Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is a neurodevelopmental condition whose early diagnosis remains challenging because conventional clinical assessments are often subjective, time-consuming, and require expert evaluation. Gait provides a promising non-invasive behavioral biomarker for auto- mated ASD screening; however, existing studies have primarily relied on single-dataset evaluations, convolutional architectures, and descriptive summaries of cross-validation performance without formally assessing fold-to-fold stability. This study addresses these gaps with an attention-enhanced Transformer framework for ASD classification, evaluated on two structurally different 3D gait feature representations: precomputed statistical gait descriptors and raw biomechanical ground-reaction- force measurements. Under five-fold cross-validation, the proposed framework achieved 99.00% accuracy, 99.02% precision, 99.00% recall, 99.00% F1-score, and 99.00% specificity on the public Kinect-based benchmark, exceeding the performance of the compared state-of-the-art methods. On the independent private force-plate dataset, it achieved mean values of 95.00% accuracy, 93.81% precision, 96.67% recall, 95.13% F1-score, and 93.33% specificity.

</details>


### [29] [What Input Resolution Is Required for Bird Species Identification, and What Is Its Latency Cost on an Edge Device? A Study of 14 Input Resolutions and Six Architectures with On-Device Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14247)
*Takeshi Nishikawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以因子实验（14种输入分辨率×6种架构×2种训练评估机制×30随机种子，共2520个检查点、5040次评估，并在Jetson Orin Nano上测延迟）系统研究鸟类撞击缓解中的输入分辨率设计问题，得出四条结论：N的选择取决于精度目标与模型；降N的收益取决于预处理路径；精度必须在部署引擎上测量（FP16会显著损伤ViT但几乎不影响CNN）；通过按transformer块边界切分计算图可将FP32限制在受影响段，使DINOv2-L链快1.85倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电场鸟类撞击缓解需要识别仅占几十像素的远距离鸟类，因此分类器输入分辨率N是设计变量而非固定规格；然而N、架构、训练/评估机制与部署精度之间缺乏系统的联合分析，尤其缺少面向实际边缘设备（Jetson Orin Nano）的延迟与数值精度考量。

Method: 采用因子设计：14种边长N（16–224）×6种架构×2种训练与评估机制×30个随机种子，共2520个检查点、5040次评估；在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上测量延迟；对比半精度（FP16）与FP32引擎精度；通过按transformer块边界切分计算图实现混合精度部署；并进行种子数量稳定性与组共享敏感性分析。

Result: (1) N依目标而定：ResNet50在N=112以约1.85 ms达验证0.90（测试0.8980），DINOv2-L在N=144以12.70 ms达0.95；换模型比提高N更有效（N=112处+5.93 vs +2.33点）。(2) 降N收益依预处理路径：N=224→80在逐文件解码时省13.5%，检测器一次解码4K帧时省46.2%；Pareto集从20增至23。(3) 精度须在部署引擎上测：FP16使ViT-S/16在N≥96损失4–7点，CNN在0.1点内；验证选择在176个目标中26个不同；错误FP16引擎可能比正确引擎更快且延迟不可辨；纳入14个ViT-S/16 FP32配置使推荐落在0.931–0.938区间且在10 ms预算内。(4) ViT-L规模模型适配设备但激活超出FP16范围；按transformer块边界切分将FP32限于受影响段，使DINOv2-L链比单引擎构建快1.85倍；并量化了机制差异符号随种子数的稳定性，敏感性拆分保留全部14个非平凡符号。

Conclusion: 输入分辨率应作为依赖精度目标、模型与部署路径的联合设计变量；精度评估必须在实际部署引擎上进行，否则半精度造成的隐性退化无法从延迟察觉；图切分混合精度策略可让ViT-L规模模型在边缘设备上高效部署；研究结论对种子数与组共享设置具有稳健性。

Abstract: Bird-strike mitigation at wind farms requires identifying distant birds that span only tens of pixels, so the classifier's input resolution N is a design variable, not a fixed specification. We study it with a factorial design over 14 side lengths N (16 to 224), six architectures, two training and evaluation regimes and 30 random seeds -- 2,520 checkpoints and 5,040 evaluations -- plus latency measured on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano. Four results. (1) The selected N depends on the target: 0.90 is met on validation by ResNet50 at N=112 in an estimated 1.85 ms (0.8980 on test) and 0.95 by DINOv2-L at N=144 in 12.70 ms; changing the model buys more accuracy than raising N (+5.93 versus +2.33 points at N=112). (2) The benefit of lowering N depends on the assumed preprocessing path: N=224 -> 80 saves 13.5% when each individual is decoded from its own file but 46.2% when the detector decodes the 4K frame once; the Pareto set grows from 20 to 23 configurations. (3) Accuracy must be measured on the deployed engine: half precision costs ViT-S/16 alone 4 to 7 points at N>=96 while the CNNs stay within 0.1 points, and with selection held at validation the choice differs at 26 of 176 targets. A broken FP16 engine can run faster than a correct one, undetectable from latency; admitting 14 ViT-S/16 FP32 configurations moves the recommendation over the 0.931-0.938 band and under the 10 ms budget. (4) ViT-L-scale models fit this device, but activations exceed the FP16 range; splitting the graph at transformer-block boundaries confines FP32 to the affected segments, making the deployed DINOv2-L chain 1.85x faster than the single-engine build. We also quantify how the regime-difference sign stabilises with seed count; a sensitivity split removing some forms of group sharing preserves all 14 non-trivial signs at the selection boundary.

</details>


### [30] [Sparsity-Adaptive Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14274)
*Shiryu Ueno,Yoshikazu Hayashi,Kunihito Kato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出稀疏自适应锐度感知最小化（SA-SAM），并评估幅度加权Hessian（MWH），在高稀疏度下提升剪枝模型的腐蚀鲁棒性且保持干净精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署需要既紧凑又对常见腐蚀鲁棒的深度神经网络，但在部署相关的高稀疏度下，标准剪枝流程常导致腐蚀鲁棒性下降，现有锐度感知训练/剪枝方法带来的鲁棒性增益有限。

Method: 提出SA-SAM，通过保持平均绝对扰动（ℓ1代理）随稀疏度增加近似不变，推导出依赖稀疏度的SAM/ASAM扰动半径；并评估作为简单补充方案的MWH，其重要性正比于Diag(F)_i|w_i|，其中Diag(F)为对角经验Fisher曲率代理。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C和ImageNet-100-C上，80–90%稀疏度下腐蚀鲁棒性优于所比较的剪枝基线，同时保持干净精度；并报告了部署相关稀疏度下稀疏执行的实测推理吞吐量以量化鲁棒性–吞吐量权衡。

Conclusion: SA-SAM与MWH可在高稀疏度下有效缓解剪枝带来的腐蚀鲁棒性退化，兼顾干净精度与推理效率，为部署场景提供实用方案。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural networks in real-world settings requires models that are both compact and robust to common corruptions. However, at deployment-relevant high sparsity, standard pruning pipelines often degrade corruption robustness, and existing sharpness-aware training/pruning approaches provide limited robustness gains. We address this issue by introducing Sparsity-Adaptive Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SA-SAM), which derives a sparsity-dependent SAM/ASAM perturbation radius by keeping the mean absolute perturbation (an $\ell_1$-based proxy) approximately invariant as sparsity increases. As a simple complementary option, we evaluate Magnitude-Weighted Hessian (MWH), derived from a second-order removal-path analysis, yielding an importance proportional to $\mathrm{Diag}(F)_i\,|w_i|$, where $\mathrm{Diag}(F)$ is the diagonal empirical Fisher used as a curvature proxy in our implementation. Across CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C, and ImageNet-100-C, our approach achieved stronger corruption robustness than the considered pruning baselines at 80--90\% sparsity, while preserving clean accuracy. We additionally quantify the robustness--throughput trade-off by reporting measured inference throughput under sparse execution at deployment-relevant sparsity levels.

</details>


### [31] [SpermYOLO: A Coordinated YOLO-Based Detector for Accurate and Efficient Sperm and Impurity Detection in Microscopic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14278)
*Shengqi Chen,Zilin Wang,Xingyu Pan,Wenting Yu,Pengchao Deng,Guohua Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpermYOLO：基于YOLOv11的紧凑协同框架，实现精子与杂质联合检测，在SVIA与SDTB基准上取得领先精度且保持轻量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微图像中精子检测面临密集分布、视觉相似伪影及类精子杂质等困难，影响计算机辅助精液分析的准确性，现有通用检测器与专用模型在该场景下精度不足。

Method: 方法为在YOLOv11基础上引入四项结构改进：C3k2-IDB（通道级判别特征提取）、D2SEM（空间-光谱语义增强）、MFM（自适应多尺度特征融合）、DESD Head（细节增强共享预测），构成协同紧凑的联合检测框架，同时输出精子与杂质类别。

Result: 在SVIA基准上取得97.2%精子AP与75.4%杂质AP；相较基线，精子AP、杂质AP、mAP50、mAP50:95分别提升1.6、10.0、5.8、2.7个百分点，且模型规模保持轻量。跨场景SDTB睾丸活检基准上，在极小精子目标与复杂组织背景下仍达到最高mAP50=74.8%、mAP50:95=31.2%。消融与定性分析验证各模块贡献，特征响应更集中于目标区域。

Conclusion: SpermYOLO在困难显微成像场景下兼顾有效性与效率，模块设计对精子/杂质判别特征与多尺度融合具有实际增益，具备跨场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate sperm detection is essential for computer-assisted semen analysis, yet it remains challenging in microscopic images due to dense distributions, visually similar artifacts, and sperm-like impurities. In this paper, we propose SpermYOLO, a coordinated and compact YOLOv11-derived framework for joint sperm and impurity detection in microscopic images. SpermYOLO introduces four architectural improvements: C3k2-IDB for channel-wise discriminative feature extraction, D2SEM for spatial--spectral semantic enhancement, MFM for adaptive multi-scale feature fusion, and the DESD Head for detail-enhanced shared prediction. Experiments on the SVIA semen microscopic imaging benchmark show that SpermYOLO achieves 97.2\% sperm AP and 75.4\% impurity AP, outperforming generic detectors, dedicated sperm detection models, and improved YOLO variants. Compared with the baseline model, SpermYOLO improves sperm AP, impurity AP, $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$, and $\mathrm{mAP}_{50:95}$ by 1.6, 10.0, 5.8, and 2.7 percentage points, respectively, while preserving a lightweight model scale. Cross-scene evaluation on the SDTB testicular-biopsy microscopy benchmark shows that SpermYOLO remains effective with extremely small sperm targets and complex tissue backgrounds, achieving the highest $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ and $\mathrm{mAP}_{50:95}$ of 74.8\% and 31.2\%, respectively. Ablation studies and qualitative analyses further support these improvements by demonstrating the contributions of the proposed modules and showing more focused feature response patterns than the baseline model. These findings suggest that SpermYOLO is an effective and efficient approach for sperm detection in challenging microscopic imaging scenarios.

</details>


### [32] [Robust low-rank tensor completion via factorized weighted tensor schatten-p norm minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14307)
*Binghao Wang,Feng Zhang,Wendong Wang,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出两种加权Schatten-p张量分解模型（WSpTFI与WSpTFII），在t-product框架下实现免SVD或带列剪枝的因子更新，并给出收敛性分析，在多类张量恢复任务中取得有竞争力的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩张量分解是处理不完整/受损多维数据的灵活框架，但现有方法存在两个问题：（1）无加权谱正则化对所有奇异分量施加统一的收缩轮廓，会过度衰减占主导的低秩分量；（2）已有分解式变体要么缺乏分量级加权，要么需要昂贵的SVD计算。因此需要既能实现分量自适应加权、又避免高成本SVD的分解模型。

Method: 在t-product框架下提出两种加权Schatten-p张量分解模型：WSpTFI基于分解式加权张量Schatten-p范数恒等式，允许因子指数灵活甚至非对称；WSpTFII由变换域列对能量构造正则项，实现免SVD的主因子更新并引入列剪枝机制以削减冗余秩分量。求解上，WSpTFI采用迭代重加权ADMM类方案，WSpTFII采用IRLS-BSUM方案。理论方面：为WSpTFI建立加权分解关系与条件性极限KKT刻画；为WSpTFII说明实际阻尼二次块更新对固定δ平滑因子目标构成定量充分下降机制，从而推出渐近正则性及固定维尾部任意聚点的驻点性。

Result: 在合成张量补全、彩色图像复原、高光谱图像修复以及印刷电路板缺陷检测实验中，两个模型在多种退化条件下均表现出有竞争力的重建质量与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提WSpTFI与WSpTFII通过分量级加权与（或）免SVD更新，缓解了统一收缩导致的低秩主导分量过度衰减问题，并具备可证明的收敛性质，在多种张量恢复任务上验证了有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Low-rank tensor factorization provides a flexible framework for completing multidimensional data from incomplete and corrupted observations. However, unweighted spectral regularizers impose a common shrinkage profile across singular components, which may excessively attenuate dominant low-rank components, and factorized variants either lack component-specific weighting or require costly singular value decompositions (SVDs). This paper proposes two weighted Schatten-$p$ tensor factorization models, termed \WSpTFI{} and \WSpTFII{}, under the tensor-tensor product (t-product) framework to address these limitations. \WSpTFI{} is motivated by a factorized weighted tensor Schatten-$p$ norm identity and permits flexible, possibly asymmetric factor exponents. \WSpTFII{} constructs a regularizer from transform-domain column-pair energies, yielding SVD-free main factor updates and a column-pruning mechanism for reducing redundant rank components. This paper further develops an iteratively reweighted alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM)-type scheme for \WSpTFI{} and an iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS)--block successive upper-bound minimization (BSUM) scheme for \WSpTFII{}. Theoretical analysis establishes the weighted factorization relation and provides a conditional limiting Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) characterization for \WSpTFI{}. For \WSpTFII{}, the actual damped quadratic block updates yield a quantitative sufficient-decrease mechanism for the fixed-$δ$ smoothed factor objective. This implies asymptotic regularity, and every accumulation point of the fixed-dimensional tail is stationary. Experiments on synthetic tensor completion, color-image restoration, hyperspectral inpainting, and printed-circuit-board defect detection demonstrate competitive reconstruction quality and robustness under various degradation conditions.

</details>


### [33] [Mobile CT Services for Rural, Regional, and Remote Areas: Current Practice and Future Integration with Telehealth and Regulatory-Authorised AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14347)
*Zhicheng Lu,Md Zahid Islam,M Mamun Huda,Kristie Sweeney,Shayne Chau,Oliver Mulcock,Corey Hemopo,Catherine Keniry,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 综述移动CT、远程医疗与CT人工智能三类技术在偏远及资源有限地区的临床应用，按筛查诊断、监测、风险预测与治疗决策四类功能评估，指出各技术相对成熟但整合部署与验证仍不足，需前瞻性多中心研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT对改善临床结局至关重要，但在农村、区域、偏远（RRR）及资源有限地区，CT成像可及性与专科解读能力仍然受限。移动CT、远程医疗和AI的新进展为向这些人群扩展先进影像服务提供了机会，因此有必要系统梳理其成熟度与部署现状。

Method: 文献综述。分别考察：1）部署于卡车、拖车、救护车等移动平台的移动CT系统；2）支持CT相关医疗的远程医疗技术；3）已获监管授权或已进入临床实践的CT人工智能。按四类临床功能（筛查与诊断、患者监测、风险预测、干预或治疗决策支持）进行评估，覆盖神经、胸部、心血管、腹部、肿瘤、肌骨及介入影像，重点关注卒中、肿瘤及其他影像引导治疗，并比较监管状态、部署状态与估计技术成熟度（TRL）等级。

Result: 现有证据表明，移动CT、远程医疗与用于常规CT的AI各自相对成熟，但三者的完全整合在临床场景中的部署与验证仍不广泛。主要障碍包括监管差异、域偏移、连通性要求、成本、工作流程整合、网络安全以及患者层面获益证据有限。

Conclusion: 未来研究应优先在真实世界及服务不足的临床环境中，对整合式CT系统开展前瞻性、多中心评估。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) plays an essential role in clinical workflow to improve patient outcomes. However, access to CT imaging and specialist interpretation remains limited, particularly in rural, regional, remote (RRR), and other resource-limited settings. Recent advances in mobile CT, telehealth, and artificial intelligence (AI) provide opportunities to extend advanced imaging services to populations in RRR settings. This review examines: 1) mobile CT systems deployed in trucks, trailers, ambulances, and other mobile platforms; 2) telehealth technologies supporting CT-based healthcare; and 3) AI for CT that has received regulatory authorisation or is currently deployed in clinical practice. Applications are evaluated across four clinical functions: screening and diagnosis, patient monitoring, risk prediction, and intervention or therapeutic decision support. The review covers neurological, thoracic, cardiovascular, abdominal, oncological, musculoskeletal, and interventional imaging, with particular attention to stroke, cancer, and other image-guided treatment. Other factors such as regulatory status, deployment status, and estimated technology readiness (TRL) level are compared. Current evidence indicates that mobile CT, telehealth, and AI for conventional CT are individually relatively mature, but fully integration of these technologies remains less widely deployed and validated in the clinical settings. Key barriers include regulatory variation, domain shift, connectivity requirements, cost, workflow integration, cybersecurity, and limited evidence of patient-level benefit. Future research should prioritise prospective, multicentre evaluation of integrated CT systems in real-world and underserved clinical settings.

</details>


### [34] [Two-Stage Mixture-of-LoRA for Multi-Task Medical Vision-Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14350)
*Zhanghao Chen,Yuanyuan Li,Zhenyu Lu,Shuo Gao,Guangquan Zhou,Yikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段混合LoRA（Two-Stage Mixture-of-LoRA）框架，基于MedGemma-1.5-4B实现医学视觉语言模型的联合适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型需在多种临床图像任务上联合适配，但面临异构输出格式、任务梯度冲突和训练数据不平衡等挑战。
